rating: 1
review_text: If someone wrote a song about a bad Italian restaurant this would be it!

The restaurants decor screams high end experience. The menus and reviews would lead you to believe this was a 4 star restaurant. Trust me it is not! Swearing bartender, rude hostess, incompetent wait staff and inedible food was our experience.

We arrived at 5:00pm on lasagna night. Understaffed? There were empty tables but over hour wait.
The contemptuous hostess seemed mad we wanted a table and didn't have a reservation. The bartender would Indulge in a bit of lalochezia anytime a large order came in. Our waiter continually botched our order.

By 6:30 they were out of happy hour wine, out of both soups, out of the lasagna. Out of it on lasagna night?
When we finally received our food at 7: 30 My lasagna (Waiter said they were making more and would be 15 minutes at 6:30) was small and hard, obviously an end scrap I would have never served to anyone. My wife's veal marsala was over cooked, salty and inedible.

For the record we were offered free salads to replace the soup and free dessert to make up for what the waiter described as a "wreck" we declined both, paid our full bill, went back to our condo and had a sandwich. We didnt come for free food. We came for a fine Italian experience with friends. Tiramisu doesn't make up for 3 hours of misery.

id: e58cb70a-f192-4ca1-b3e8-e9ece9063423
entity_slug: ristorante-carmelo
reviewer_name: H. Hays
rating: 1
body: If someone wrote a song about a bad Italian restaurant this would be it!

The restaurants decor screams high end experience. The menus and reviews would lead you to believe this was a 4 star restaurant. Trust me it is not! Swearing bartender, rude hostess, incompetent wait staff and inedible food was our experience.

We arrived at 5:00pm on lasagna night. Understaffed? There were empty tables but over hour wait.
The contemptuous hostess seemed mad we wanted a table and didn't have a reservation. The bartender would Indulge in a bit of lalochezia anytime a large order came in. Our waiter continually botched our order.

By 6:30 they were out of happy hour wine, out of both soups, out of the lasagna. Out of it on lasagna night?
When we finally received our food at 7: 30 My lasagna (Waiter said they were making more and would be 15 minutes at 6:30) was small and hard, obviously an end scrap I would have never served to anyone. My wife's veal marsala was over cooked, salty and inedible.

For the record we were offered free salads to replace the soup and free dessert to make up for what the waiter described as a "wreck" we declined both, paid our full bill, went back to our condo and had a sandwich. We didnt come for free food. We came for a fine Italian experience with friends. Tiramisu doesn't make up for 3 hours of misery.
